## 1. camera_obj_rel_dist_v1: sb_rowB3_44bdfe365a7f834330a26fc4

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (shelf, stove, sink, refrigerator) is the closest to the camera in frame 5 of 32?

A. shelf
B. stove
C. sink
D. refrigerator

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 5 of 32. Sighting references use the latest recorded instance sighting before this frame, or the nearest recorded sighting if none is earlier.
Step 2: the object labelled "shelf" is in view in frame 5; relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the stove.
Step 3: the object labelled "stove" is in view in frame 5; relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the sink and nearer than the shelf.
Step 4: the object labelled "sink" is in view in frame 5; relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the refrigerator and nearer than the stove.
Step 5: the object labelled "refrigerator" is in view in frame 5; relative to that camera, its closest point is nearer than the sink.
Step 6: ordered nearest to farthest from that camera, the objects are refrigerator, then sink, then stove, then shelf.
So the closest object to that camera is the refrigerator.
</think>

D

## 2. camera_obj_rel_dist_v1: sb_rowB3_f3d63b10189169be23233110

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (refrigerator, shelf, stove, washer) is the closest to the camera in frame 30 of 32?

A. refrigerator
B. shelf
C. stove
D. washer

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 30 of 32. Sighting references use the latest recorded instance sighting before this frame, or the nearest recorded sighting if none is earlier.
Step 2: the object labelled "refrigerator" is out of view in frame 30; it was last seen in frame 2 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the shelf.
Step 3: the object labelled "shelf" is out of view in frame 30; it was last seen in frame 12 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the washer and nearer than the refrigerator.
Step 4: the object labelled "stove" is out of view in frame 30; it was last seen in frame 2 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is nearer than the washer.
Step 5: the object labelled "washer" is out of view in frame 30; it was last seen in frame 25 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the stove and nearer than the shelf.
Step 6: ordered nearest to farthest from that camera, the objects are stove, then washer, then shelf, then refrigerator.
So the closest object to that camera is the stove.
</think>

C

## 3. camera_obj_rel_dist_v1: sb_rowB3_15cff71f124c78796a5bedfb

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (fireplace, stove, refrigerator, sink) is the closest to the camera in frame 18 of 32?

A. fireplace
B. stove
C. refrigerator
D. sink

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 18 of 32. Sighting references use the latest recorded instance sighting before this frame, or the nearest recorded sighting if none is earlier.
Step 2: the object labelled "fireplace" is in view in frame 18; relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the refrigerator.
Step 3: the object labelled "stove" is out of view in frame 18; it was last seen in frame 3 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the sink and nearer than the refrigerator.
Step 4: the object labelled "refrigerator" is out of view in frame 18; it was last seen in frame 4 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the stove and nearer than the fireplace.
Step 5: the object labelled "sink" is out of view in frame 18; it was last seen in frame 3 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is nearer than the stove.
Step 6: ordered nearest to farthest from that camera, the objects are sink, then stove, then refrigerator, then fireplace.
So the closest object to that camera is the sink.
</think>

D

## 4. camera_obj_rel_dist_v1: sb_rowB3_1f96314fc49b707b63320b30

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (tv, table, oven, sink) is the closest to the camera in frame 18 of 32?

A. tv
B. table
C. oven
D. sink

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 18 of 32. Sighting references use the latest recorded instance sighting before this frame, or the nearest recorded sighting if none is earlier.
Step 2: the object labelled "tv" is out of view in frame 18; it was last seen in frame 24 (nearest recorded sighting, later in the clip); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the oven and nearer than the sink.
Step 3: the object labelled "table" is in view in frame 18; relative to that camera, its closest point is nearer than the oven.
Step 4: the object labelled "oven" is out of view in frame 18; it was last seen in frame 19 (nearest recorded sighting, later in the clip); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the table and nearer than the tv.
Step 5: the object labelled "sink" is out of view in frame 18; it was last seen in frame 19 (nearest recorded sighting, later in the clip); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the tv.
Step 6: ordered nearest to farthest from that camera, the objects are table, then oven, then tv, then sink.
So the closest object to that camera is the table.
</think>

B

## 5. camera_obj_rel_dist_v1: sb_rowB3_07bb9696e83eaf432762aae6

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (watering can, books, folded tablecloth, window) is the closest to the camera in frame 16 of 32?

A. watering can
B. books
C. folded tablecloth
D. window

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 16 of 32. Sighting references use the latest recorded instance sighting before this frame, or the nearest recorded sighting if none is earlier.
Step 2: the object labelled "watering can" is out of view in frame 16; it was last seen in frame 3 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the window.
Step 3: the object labelled "books" is out of view in frame 16; it was last seen in frame 14 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is nearer than the folded tablecloth.
Step 4: the object labelled "folded tablecloth" is out of view in frame 16; it was last seen in frame 12 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the books and nearer than the window.
Step 5: the object labelled "window" is out of view in frame 16; it was last seen in frame 7 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the folded tablecloth and nearer than the watering can.
Step 6: ordered nearest to farthest from that camera, the objects are books, then folded tablecloth, then window, then watering can.
So the closest object to that camera is the books.
</think>

B

## 7. camera_obj_rel_dist_v2: sb_rowB3_07fe6374992a9273fa3aa971

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (shelf, bathtub, toilet) is the closest to the camera in frame 28 of 32?

A. shelf
B. bathtub
C. toilet

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 28 of 32. Sighting references use the latest recorded instance sighting before this frame, or the nearest recorded sighting if none is earlier.
Step 2: the object labelled "shelf" is out of view in frame 28; it was last seen in frame 10 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the toilet.
Step 3: the object labelled "bathtub" is out of view in frame 28; it was last seen in frame 10 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is nearer than the toilet.
Step 4: the object labelled "toilet" is out of view in frame 28; it was last seen in frame 10 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the bathtub and nearer than the shelf.
Step 5: ordered nearest to farthest from that camera, the objects are bathtub, then toilet, then shelf.
So the closest object to that camera is the bathtub.
</think>

B

## 8. camera_obj_rel_dist_v2: sb_rowB3_9631ac08bc54a60a79ff3594

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (table, chair, cabinet) is the closest to the camera in frame 24 of 32?

A. table
B. chair
C. cabinet

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 24 of 32. Sighting references use the latest recorded instance sighting before this frame, or the nearest recorded sighting if none is earlier.
Step 2: the object labelled "table" is out of view in frame 24; it was last seen in frame 3 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the cabinet and nearer than the chair.
Step 3: the object labelled "chair" is in view in frame 24; relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the table.
Step 4: the object labelled "cabinet" is in view in frame 24; relative to that camera, its closest point is nearer than the table.
Step 5: ordered nearest to farthest from that camera, the objects are cabinet, then table, then chair.
So the closest object to that camera is the cabinet.
</think>

C

## 9. camera_obj_rel_dist_v2: sb_rowB3_f532d166a7d40a830fd8e7b7

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (sofa, washer, oven) is the closest to the camera in frame 1 of 32?

A. sofa
B. washer
C. oven

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 1 of 32. Sighting references use the latest recorded instance sighting before this frame, or the nearest recorded sighting if none is earlier.
Step 2: the object labelled "sofa" is in view in frame 1; relative to that camera, its closest point is nearer than the washer.
Step 3: the object labelled "washer" is out of view in frame 1; it was last seen in frame 4 (nearest recorded sighting, later in the clip); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the sofa and nearer than the oven.
Step 4: the object labelled "oven" is out of view in frame 1; it was last seen in frame 4 (nearest recorded sighting, later in the clip); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the washer.
Step 5: ordered nearest to farthest from that camera, the objects are sofa, then washer, then oven.
So the closest object to that camera is the sofa.
</think>

A

Step 3: the object labelled "tv" is out of view in frame 28; it was last seen in frame 18 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is nearer than the chair.
Step 4: the object labelled "chair" is out of view in frame 28; it was last seen in frame 1 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the tv and nearer than the sink.
Step 5: ordered nearest to farthest from that camera, the objects are tv, then chair, then sink.
So the closest object to that camera is the tv.
</think>

B

## 12. camera_obj_rel_dist_v3: sb_rowB3_cf72e3372cfef1e7e5101b2d

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (bathtub, table) is the closest to the camera in frame 26 of 32?

A. bathtub
B. table

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 26 of 32. Sighting references use the latest recorded instance sighting before this frame, or the nearest recorded sighting if none is earlier.
Step 2: the object labelled "bathtub" is out of view in frame 26; it was last seen in frame 2 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is nearer than the table.
Step 3: the object labelled "table" is out of view in frame 26; it was last seen in frame 2 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the bathtub.
Step 4: ordered nearest to farthest from that camera, the objects are bathtub, then table.
So the closest object to that camera is the bathtub.
</think>

A

## 15. camera_obj_rel_dist_v3: sb_rowB3_bd32fca0ff553ed6967db666

Measuring from the closest point of each object, which of these objects (fireplace, shelf) is the closest to the camera in frame 24 of 32?

A. fireplace
B. shelf

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 24 of 32. Sighting references use the latest recorded instance sighting before this frame, or the nearest recorded sighting if none is earlier.
Step 2: the object labelled "fireplace" is out of view in frame 24; it was last seen in frame 1 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the shelf.
Step 3: the object labelled "shelf" is out of view in frame 24; it was last seen in frame 2 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is nearer than the fireplace.
Step 4: ordered nearest to farthest from that camera, the objects are shelf, then fireplace.
So the closest object to that camera is the shelf.
</think>

B

## 16. obj_obj_relative_pos_lr: sb_rowB3_6ba0acc8128d3b0d72d1b2ea

In frame 7 of 32, relative to toilet, is sink to the [Left/Right]?

A. Right
B. Left

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 7 of 32. Sighting references use the latest recorded instance sighting before this frame, or the nearest recorded sighting if none is earlier.
Step 2: the object labelled "sink" is in view in frame 7; from that camera viewpoint, its centre is to the left of the centre of the toilet.
Step 3: the object labelled "toilet" is in view in frame 7; from that camera viewpoint, its centre is to the right of the centre of the sink.
So from that camera viewpoint, the centre of the sink is to the left of the centre of the toilet.
</think>

B

## 20. obj_obj_relative_pos_lr: sb_rowB3_ac03bf06c6ca8a45db1c1b7f

In frame 3 of 32, relative to toilet, is bathtub to the [Left/Right]?

A. Right
B. Left

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 3 of 32. Sighting references use the latest recorded instance sighting before this frame, or the nearest recorded sighting if none is earlier.
Step 2: the object labelled "bathtub" is in view in frame 3; from that camera viewpoint, its centre is to the right of the centre of the toilet.
Step 3: the object labelled "toilet" is out of view in frame 3; it was last seen in frame 6 (nearest recorded sighting, later in the clip); from that camera viewpoint, its centre is to the left of the centre of the bathtub.
So from that camera viewpoint, the centre of the bathtub is to the right of the centre of the toilet.
</think>

A

B. Near

Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 9 of 32. Sighting references use the latest recorded instance sighting before this frame, or the nearest recorded sighting if none is earlier.
Step 2: the object labelled "bed" is in view in frame 9; relative to that camera, its closest point is nearer than the closest point of the fireplace.
Step 3: the object labelled "fireplace" is out of view in frame 9; it was last seen in frame 3 (latest recorded sighting before the queried frame); relative to that camera, its closest point is farther than the closest point of the bed.

In frame 23 of 32, relative to table, is shelf to the [Up/Down]?

A. Down
B. Up

Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 23 of 32. Sighting references use the latest recorded instance sighting before this frame, or the nearest recorded sighting if none is earlier.

Step 3: the object labelled "table" is in view in frame 23; in that camera's level horizon, its centre is lower than the centre of the shelf.

## 28. obj_obj_relative_pos_ud: sb_rowB3_3f26f685f99b19407a909782

In frame 6 of 32, relative to chair, is curtain to the [Up/Down]?

A. Down
B. Up

<think>
Step 1: take the camera viewpoint of frame 6 of 32. Sighting references use the latest recorded instance sighting before this frame, or the nearest recorded sighting if none is earlier.
Step 2: the object labelled "curtain" is in view in frame 6; in that camera's level horizon, its centre is higher than the centre of the chair.
Step 3: the object labelled "chair" is out of view in frame 6; it was last seen in frame 13 (nearest recorded sighting, later in the clip); in that camera's level horizon, its centre is lower than the centre of the curtain.
So the centre of the curtain is higher than the centre of the chair in that camera's level horizon.
</think>

B
